```
   199→def test_show_post_scan_analysis_surfaces_warnings_and_headline(monkeypatch, capsys):
   200→    import desloppify.intelligence.narrative as narrative_mod
   201→
   202→    monkeypatch.setattr(
   203→        narrative_mod,
   204→        "compute_narrative",
   205→        lambda *_args, **_kwargs: {
   206→            "headline": "Tighten structural debt first",
   207→            "strategy": {
   208→                "hint": "Parallelize auto-fixers and resolve highest tier findings first",
   209→                "can_parallelize": True,
   210→            },
   211→            "actions": [
   212→                {
   213→                    "command": "desloppify next",
   214→                    "description": "Fix highest priority finding",
   215→                }
   216→            ],
   217→        },
   218→    )
   219→
   220→    warnings, narrative = scan_reporting_analysis_mod.show_post_scan_analysis(
   221→        diff={
   222→            "new": 12,
   223→            "auto_resolved": 1,
   224→            "reopened": 6,
   225→            "chronic_reopeners": ["x", "y"],
   226→        },
   227→        state={"findings": {}, "scan_path": ".", "review_cache": {"files": {}}},
   228→        lang=SimpleNamespace(name="python"),
   229→    )
   230→    out = capsys.readouterr().out
   231→    assert any("reopened" in warning for warning in warnings)
   232→    assert any("cascading" in warning.lower() for warning in warnings)
   233→    assert any("chronic reopener" in warning.lower() for warning in warnings)
   234→    # Slimmed scan: headline + pointers only (no Agent Plan, no review nudge)
   235→    assert "AGENT PLAN" not in out
   236→    assert "Tighten structural debt first" in out
   237→    assert "desloppify next" in out
   238→    assert "desloppify status" in out
   239→    assert narrative["headline"] == "Tighten structural debt first"
   240→

   374→def test_print_llm_summary_respects_env_and_includes_dimension_table(
   375→    monkeypatch,
   376→    capsys,
   377→    tmp_path,
   378→):
   379→    import desloppify.core.registry as registry_mod
   380→    import desloppify.scoring as scoring_mod
   381→    import desloppify.state as state_mod
   382→
   383→    monkeypatch.setenv("DESLOPPIFY_AGENT", "1")
   384→    monkeypatch.delenv("CLAUDE_CODE", raising=False)
   385→    monkeypatch.setattr(state_mod, "get_overall_score", lambda _state: 91.0)
   386→    monkeypatch.setattr(state_mod, "get_objective_score", lambda _state: 90.0)
   387→    monkeypatch.setattr(state_mod, "get_strict_score", lambda _state: 88.0)
   388→    monkeypatch.setattr(state_mod, "get_verified_strict_score", lambda _state: 87.0)
   389→    monkeypatch.setattr(
   390→        scoring_mod,
   391→        "DIMENSIONS",
   392→        [
   393→            SimpleNamespace(name="File health"),
   394→            SimpleNamespace(name="Code quality"),
   395→        ],
   396→    )
   397→    monkeypatch.setattr(registry_mod, "dimension_action_type", lambda _name: "fix")
   398→
   399→    badge_path = Path(tmp_path / "badge.png")
   400→    badge_path.write_bytes(b"x")
   401→    state = {
   402→        "dimension_scores": {
   403→            "File health": {
```

```
    23→class TestFormatDetail:
    24→    """format_detail builds display-ready parts from a finding detail dict."""
    25→
    26→    def test_empty_detail(self):
    27→        assert format_detail({}) == []
    28→
    29→    def test_simple_string_fields(self):
    30→        parts = format_detail({"category": "imports", "kind": "default"})
    31→        assert "category: imports" in parts
    32→        assert "kind: default" in parts
    33→
    34→    def test_line_number(self):
    35→        parts = format_detail({"line": 42})
    36→        assert "line: 42" in parts
    37→
    38→    def test_lines_list_truncated(self):
    39→        parts = format_detail({"lines": [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7]})
    40→        # Only first 5 should appear
    41→        lines_part = [p for p in parts if p.startswith("lines:")]
    42→        assert len(lines_part) == 1
    43→        assert "6" not in lines_part[0]
    44→        assert "1" in lines_part[0]
    45→
    46→    def test_signals_list(self):
    47→        parts = format_detail({"signals": ["a", "b", "c", "d"]})
    48→        sig_part = [p for p in parts if p.startswith("signals:")][0]
    49→        # Only first 3
    50→        assert "a" in sig_part
    51→        assert "c" in sig_part
    52→        assert "d" not in sig_part
    53→
    54→    def test_importers_zero_is_shown(self):
    55→        """importers=0 is meaningful and should be displayed."""
    56→        parts = format_detail({"importers": 0})
    57→        assert "importers: 0" in parts
    58→
    59→    def test_importers_none_is_hidden(self):
    60→        """importers=None should not show up."""
    61→        parts = format_detail({"importers": None})
    62→        importers_parts = [p for p in parts if "importers" in p]
    63→        assert importers_parts == []
    64→
    65→    def test_count_zero_is_hidden(self):
    66→        """count=0 is not meaningful and should be skipped."""
    67→        parts = format_detail({"count": 0})
    68→        count_parts = [p for p in parts if "count" in p]
    69→        assert count_parts == []
    70→
    71→    def test_review_truncated_at_80(self):
    72→        long_review = "x" * 200
    73→        parts = format_detail({"review": long_review})
    74→        review_part = [p for p in parts if p.startswith("review:")][0]
    75→        # Formatter truncates to 80 chars

   113→        assert "useToolSettings:2 file(s)" in ev_part

   115→    def test_outliers_truncated(self):

   126→class TestBuildShowPayload:
   127→    """build_show_payload produces structured JSON for query and --output."""
   128→
   129→    def _make_finding(
   130→        self, fid, *, file="a.ts", detector="unused", tier=2, confidence="high"
   131→    ):

   133→            "id": fid,
   134→            "file": file,

   142→    def test_empty_matches(self):

   144→        assert result["total"] == 0

   151→class TestSuppressedMatchEstimate:
   152→    def _make_finding(self, fid, *, file="a.ts", detector="unused",
   153→                      tier=2, confidence="high"):
   154→        return {
   155→            "id": fid, "file": file, "detector": detector,
   156→            "tier": tier, "confidence": confidence,
   157→            "summary": f"Finding {fid}", "detail": {},
   158→        }
   159→
   160→    def test_detector_name(self):
   161→        assert suppressed_match_estimate("smells", {"smells": 4}) == 4
   162→
   163→    def test_detector_prefix_pattern(self):
   164→        assert suppressed_match_estimate("smells::*::x", {"smells": 7}) == 7
   165→
   166→    def test_unknown_pattern_returns_zero(self):
   167→        assert suppressed_match_estimate("nope", {"smells": 3}) == 0
   168→
   169→    def test_single_finding(self):
   170→        findings = [self._make_finding("unused::a.ts::foo")]
   171→        result = build_show_payload(findings, "a.ts", "open")
   172→        assert result["total"] == 1
   173→        assert result["summary"]["files"] == 1
   174→        assert result["summary"]["by_tier"] == {"T2": 1}
   175→        assert result["summary"]["by_detector"] == {"unused": 1}
   176→        assert "a.ts" in result["by_file"]
   177→        assert len(result["by_file"]["a.ts"]) == 1
   178→
   179→    def test_multiple_files_and_detectors(self):
   180→        findings = [
   181→            self._make_finding(
   182→                "unused::a.ts::foo", file="a.ts", detector="unused", tier=2
   183→            ),
   184→            self._make_finding(
   185→                "smells::b.ts::bar", file="b.ts", detector="smells", tier=3
   186→            ),
   187→            self._make_finding(
   188→                "unused::a.ts::baz", file="a.ts", detector="unused", tier=2
   189→            ),
   190→        ]
   191→        result = build_show_payload(findings, "*", "open")
   192→        assert result["total"] == 3
   193→        assert result["summary"]["files"] == 2
   194→        assert result["summary"]["by_tier"] == {"T2": 2, "T3": 1}
   195→        assert result["summary"]["by_detector"]["unused"] == 2
   196→        assert result["summary"]["by_detector"]["smells"] == 1
   197→
   198→    def test_by_file_sorted_by_count_descending(self):
   199→        findings = [
   200→            self._make_finding("a1", file="a.ts"),
   201→            self._make_finding("a2", file="a.ts"),
   202→            self._make_finding("a3", file="a.ts"),
   203→            self._make_finding("b1", file="b.ts"),
   204→        ]
   205→        result = build_show_payload(findings, "*", "open")
   206→        files = list(result["by_file"].keys())
   207→        # a.ts has 3 findings, b.ts has 1 -- a.ts should come first
   208→        assert files[0] == "a.ts"
   209→
   210→    def test_by_detector_sorted_by_count_descending(self):
   211→        findings = [
   212→            self._make_finding("a1", detector="alpha"),
   213→            self._make_finding("a2", detector="alpha"),
   214→            self._make_finding("b1", detector="beta"),
   215→        ]
   216→        result = build_show_payload(findings, "*", "open")
   217→        dets = list(result["summary"]["by_detector"].keys())
   218→        assert dets[0] == "alpha"
   219→
   220→    def test_payload_includes_hidden_metadata_when_provided(self):
   221→        findings = [self._make_finding("a1", detector="alpha")]
   222→        result = build_show_payload(
   223→            findings,
   224→            "*",
   225→            "open",
   226→            meta=ShowPayloadMeta(
   227→                total_matches=4,
   228→                hidden_by_detector={"alpha": 3},
   229→                noise_budget=1,
   230→                global_noise_budget=2,
   231→            ),
   232→        )
   233→        assert result["total"] == 1
   234→        assert result["total_matching"] == 4
   235→        assert result["hidden"]["total"] == 3
   236→        assert result["hidden"]["by_detector"] == {"alpha": 3}
   237→        assert result["noise_budget"] == 1
   238→        assert result["noise_global_budget"] == 2
```

```
1→"""Tests for desloppify.plan — plan generation, tier sections, and next-item priority."""

    20→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    21→# Helpers

    23→
    24→
    25→def _finding(
    26→    fid,
    27→    *,
    28→    detector="det",
    29→    file="a.py",
    30→    tier=1,
    31→    confidence="high",
    32→    summary="something wrong",
    33→    status="open",
    34→    detail=None,
    35→    note=None,
    36→):
    37→    """Build a minimal finding dict."""
    38→    return {
    39→        "id": fid,
    40→        "detector": detector,
    41→        "file": file,
    42→        "tier": tier,
    43→        "confidence": confidence,
    44→        "summary": summary,
    45→        "status": status,
    46→        "detail": detail or {},
    47→        "note": note,
    48→    }
    49→
    50→
    51→def _state(
    52→    findings_list=None,
    53→    *,
    54→    overall_score=None,
    55→    objective_score=None,
    56→    strict_score=None,
    57→    stats=None,
    58→    dimension_scores=None,
    59→    codebase_metrics=None,
    60→    subjective_assessments=None,
    61→):
    62→    """Build a minimal state dict."""
    63→    findings = {}
    64→    for f in findings_list or []:
    65→        findings[f["id"]] = f
    66→    return {
    67→        "overall_score": overall_score,
    68→        "objective_score": objective_score,
    69→        "strict_score": strict_score,
    70→        "stats": stats or {},
    71→        "findings": findings,
    72→        "dimension_scores": dimension_scores or {},
    73→        "codebase_metrics": codebase_metrics or {},
    74→        "subjective_assessments": subjective_assessments or {},
    75→    }
    76→
    77→

    80→# ===========================================================================
    81→

    83→class TestConstants:

    85→        assert set(TIER_LABELS.keys()) == {1, 2, 3, 4}
    86→
    87→    def test_confidence_order_ranking(self):
    88→        assert CONFIDENCE_ORDER["high"] < CONFIDENCE_ORDER["medium"]
    89→        assert CONFIDENCE_ORDER["medium"] < CONFIDENCE_ORDER["low"]

   100→        lines = _plan_header(st, {})
   101→        header = lines[0]
   102→        assert date.today().isoformat() in header
   103→
   104→    def test_objective_score_format(self):
   105→        st = _state(overall_score=90.0, objective_score=87.5, strict_score=82.3)
   106→        lines = _plan_header(st, {})

   114→        lines = _plan_header(st, {})

   116→        assert "Score: 42.0/100" in score_line
```

```
    29→class ScoreBundle:
    30→    dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    31→    strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]
    32→    verified_strict_dimension_scores: dict[str, dict]

    34→    objective_score: float

    39→def compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
    40→    findings: dict,
    41→    potentials: dict[str, int],
    42→    *,
    43→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
    44→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    45→) -> dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]]:
    46→    """Compute dimension scores for lenient/strict/verified_strict in one pass."""
    47→    results: dict[ScoreMode, dict[str, dict]] = {mode: {} for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    48→
    49→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    50→        totals = {
    51→            mode: {
    52→                "checks": 0,
    53→                "issues": 0,
    54→                "weighted_failures": 0.0,
    55→                "detectors": {},
    56→            }
    57→            for mode in SCORING_MODES
    58→        }
    59→
    60→        for detector in dim.detectors:
    61→            potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
    62→            if potential <= 0:
    63→                continue
    64→
    65→            detector_stats = detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)
    66→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    67→                pass_rate, issues, weighted = detector_stats[mode]
    68→                totals[mode]["checks"] += potential
    69→                totals[mode]["issues"] += issues
    70→                totals[mode]["weighted_failures"] += weighted
    71→                totals[mode]["detectors"][detector] = {
    72→                    "potential": potential,
    73→                    "pass_rate": pass_rate,
    74→                    "issues": issues,
    75→                    "weighted_failures": weighted,
    76→                }
    77→
    78→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    79→            total_checks = totals[mode]["checks"]
    80→            if total_checks <= 0:
    81→                continue
    82→            dim_score = (
    83→                max(
    84→                    0.0,
    85→                    (total_checks - totals[mode]["weighted_failures"]) / total_checks,
    86→                )
    87→                * 100
    88→            )
    89→            results[mode][dim.name] = {
    90→                "score": round(dim_score, 1),
    91→                "tier": dim.tier,
    92→                "checks": total_checks,
    93→                "issues": totals[mode]["issues"],
    94→                "detectors": totals[mode]["detectors"],
    95→            }
    96→
    97→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    98→        append_subjective_dimensions(
    99→            results[mode],
   100→            findings,
   101→            subjective_assessments,
   102→            FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode],
   103→            allowed_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   104→        )
   105→    return results
   106→
   107→
   108→def compute_dimension_scores(
   109→    findings: dict,
   110→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   111→    *,
   112→    strict: bool = False,
   113→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   114→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   115→) -> dict[str, dict]:
   116→    """Compute per-dimension scores from findings and potentials."""
   117→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   118→    return compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   119→        findings,
   120→        potentials,
   121→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   122→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   123→    )[mode]
   124→
   125→
   126→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
   127→    return " ".join(str(name).strip().lower().split())
   128→
   129→
   130→def _mechanical_dimension_weight(name: str) -> float:
   131→    return float(
   132→        MECHANICAL_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   133→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   134→            1.0,
   135→        )
   136→    )
   137→
   138→
   139→def _subjective_dimension_weight(name: str, data: dict) -> float:
   140→    subjective_meta = (
   141→        data.get("detectors", {}).get("subjective_assessment", {})
   142→        if isinstance(data, dict)
   143→        else {}
   144→    )
   145→    configured = (
   146→        subjective_meta.get("configured_weight")
   147→        if isinstance(subjective_meta, dict)
   148→        else None
   149→    )
   150→    if is_numeric(configured):
   151→        return max(0.0, float(configured))
   152→
   153→    return float(
   154→        SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSION_WEIGHTS.get(
   155→            _normalize_dimension_name(name),
   156→            1.0,
   157→        )
   158→    )
   159→
   160→
   161→def compute_health_breakdown(
   162→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   163→) -> dict[str, object]:
   164→    """Return pool averages and weighted contribution breakdown for score transparency."""
   165→    if not dimension_scores:
   166→        return {
   167→            "overall_score": 100.0,
   168→            "mechanical_fraction": 1.0,
   169→            "subjective_fraction": 0.0,
   170→            "mechanical_avg": 100.0,
   171→            "subjective_avg": None,
   172→            "entries": [],
   173→        }
   174→
   175→    mech_sum = 0.0
   176→    mech_weight = 0.0
   177→    subj_sum = 0.0
   178→    subj_weight = 0.0
   179→    mechanical_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   180→    subjective_rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   181→
   182→    for name, data in dimension_scores.items():
   183→        score = float(data.get(score_key, data.get("score", 0.0)))
   184→        is_subjective = "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
   185→        if is_subjective:
   186→            configured = max(0.0, _subjective_dimension_weight(name, data))
   187→            effective = configured
   188→            subj_sum += score * effective
   189→            subj_weight += effective
   190→            subjective_rows.append(
   191→                {
   192→                    "name": str(name),
   193→                    "score": score,
   194→                    "configured_weight": configured,
   195→                    "effective_weight": effective,
   196→                }
   197→            )
   198→            continue
   199→
   200→        checks = float(data.get("checks", 0) or 0)
   201→        sample_factor = min(1.0, checks / MIN_SAMPLE) if checks > 0 else 0.0
   202→        configured = max(0.0, _mechanical_dimension_weight(name))
   203→        effective = configured * sample_factor
   204→        mech_sum += score * effective
   205→        mech_weight += effective
   206→        mechanical_rows.append(
   207→            {
   208→                "name": str(name),
   209→                "score": score,
   210→                "checks": checks,
   211→                "sample_factor": sample_factor,
   212→                "configured_weight": configured,
   213→                "effective_weight": effective,
   214→            }
   215→        )
   216→
   217→    mech_avg = (mech_sum / mech_weight) if mech_weight > 0 else 100.0
   218→    subj_avg = (subj_sum / subj_weight) if subj_weight > 0 else None
   219→
   220→    if subj_avg is None:
   221→        mechanical_fraction = 1.0
   222→        subjective_fraction = 0.0
   223→        overall_score = round(mech_avg, 1)
   224→    elif mech_weight == 0:
   225→        mechanical_fraction = 0.0
   226→        subjective_fraction = 1.0
   227→        overall_score = round(subj_avg, 1)
   228→    else:
   229→        mechanical_fraction = MECHANICAL_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   230→        subjective_fraction = SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHT_FRACTION
   231→        overall_score = round(
   232→            mech_avg * mechanical_fraction + subj_avg * subjective_fraction,
   233→            1,
   234→        )
   235→
   236→    entries: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
   237→
   238→    for row in mechanical_rows:
   239→        pool_share = (
   240→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / mech_weight if mech_weight > 0 else 0.0
   241→        )
   242→        per_point = mechanical_fraction * pool_share
   243→        score = float(row["score"])
   244→        entries.append(
   245→            {
   246→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   247→                "pool": "mechanical",
   248→                "score": score,
   249→                "checks": float(row["checks"]),
   250→                "sample_factor": float(row["sample_factor"]),
   251→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   252→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   253→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   254→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   255→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   256→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   257→            }
   258→        )
   259→
   260→    for row in subjective_rows:
   261→        pool_share = (
   262→            float(row["effective_weight"]) / subj_weight if subj_weight > 0 else 0.0
   263→        )
   264→        per_point = subjective_fraction * pool_share
   265→        score = float(row["score"])
   266→        entries.append(
   267→            {
   268→                "name": str(row["name"]),
   269→                "pool": "subjective",
   270→                "score": score,
   271→                "checks": 0.0,
   272→                "sample_factor": 1.0,
   273→                "configured_weight": float(row["configured_weight"]),
   274→                "effective_weight": float(row["effective_weight"]),
   275→                "pool_share": pool_share,
   276→                "overall_per_point": per_point,
   277→                "overall_contribution": per_point * score,
   278→                "overall_drag": per_point * (100.0 - score),
   279→            }
   280→        )
   281→
   282→    return {
   283→        "overall_score": overall_score,
   284→        "mechanical_fraction": mechanical_fraction,
   285→        "subjective_fraction": subjective_fraction,
   286→        "mechanical_avg": mech_avg,
   287→        "subjective_avg": subj_avg,
   288→        "entries": entries,
   289→    }
   290→
   291→
   292→def compute_health_score(
   293→    dimension_scores: dict, *, score_key: str = "score"
   294→) -> float:
   295→    """Budget-weighted blend of mechanical and subjective dimension scores."""
   296→    return float(
   297→        compute_health_breakdown(dimension_scores, score_key=score_key)[
   298→            "overall_score"
   299→        ]
   300→    )
   301→
   302→
   303→def compute_score_bundle(
   304→    findings: dict,
   305→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   306→    *,
   307→    subjective_assessments: dict | None = None,
   308→    allowed_subjective_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
   309→) -> ScoreBundle:
   310→    """Compute all score channels from one scoring engine pass."""
   311→    by_mode = compute_dimension_scores_by_mode(
   312→        findings,
   313→        potentials,
   314→        subjective_assessments=subjective_assessments,
   315→        allowed_subjective_dimensions=allowed_subjective_dimensions,
   316→    )
   317→
   318→    lenient_scores = by_mode["lenient"]
   319→    strict_scores = by_mode["strict"]
   320→    verified_strict_scores = by_mode["verified_strict"]
   321→
   322→    mechanical_lenient_scores = {
   323→        name: data
   324→        for name, data in lenient_scores.items()
   325→        if "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
   326→    }
   327→
   328→    return ScoreBundle(
   329→        dimension_scores=lenient_scores,
   330→        strict_dimension_scores=strict_scores,
   331→        verified_strict_dimension_scores=verified_strict_scores,
   332→        overall_score=compute_health_score(lenient_scores),
   333→        objective_score=compute_health_score(mechanical_lenient_scores),
   334→        strict_score=compute_health_score(strict_scores),
   335→        verified_strict_score=compute_health_score(verified_strict_scores),
   336→    )
   337→
   338→
   339→def compute_score_impact(
   340→    dimension_scores: dict,
   341→    potentials: dict[str, int],
   342→    detector: str,
   343→    issues_to_fix: int,
   344→) -> float:
   345→    """Estimate score improvement from fixing N issues in a detector."""
   346→    target_dim = None
   347→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
   348→        if detector in dim.detectors:
   349→            target_dim = dim
   350→            break
   351→    if target_dim is None or target_dim.name not in dimension_scores:
   352→        return 0.0
   353→
   354→    potential = potentials.get(detector, 0)
   355→    if potential <= 0:
   356→        return 0.0
   357→
   358→    dim_data = dimension_scores[target_dim.name]
   359→    old_score = compute_health_score(dimension_scores)
   360→
   361→    det_data = dim_data["detectors"].get(detector)
   362→    if not det_data:
   363→        return 0.0
   364→
   365→    old_weighted = det_data["weighted_failures"]
   366→    new_weighted = max(0.0, old_weighted - issues_to_fix * 1.0)
   367→
   368→    total_potential = 0
   369→    total_new_weighted_failures = 0.0
   370→    for det in target_dim.detectors:
   371→        det_values = dim_data["detectors"].get(det)
   372→        if not det_values:
   373→            continue
   374→        total_potential += det_values["potential"]
   375→        if det == detector:
   376→            total_new_weighted_failures += new_weighted
   377→        else:
   378→            total_new_weighted_failures += det_values["weighted_failures"]
   379→    if total_potential <= 0:
   380→        return 0.0
   381→
   382→    new_dim_score = (
   383→        max(
   384→            0.0,
   385→            (total_potential - total_new_weighted_failures) / total_potential,
   386→        )
   387→        * 100
   388→    )
   389→
   390→    simulated = {k: dict(v) for k, v in dimension_scores.items()}
   391→    simulated[target_dim.name] = {**dim_data, "score": round(new_dim_score, 1)}
   392→    new_score = compute_health_score(simulated)
   393→
   394→    return round(new_score - old_score, 1)
   395→
   396→
   397→def get_dimension_for_detector(detector: str) -> Dimension | None:
   398→    """Look up which dimension a detector belongs to."""
   399→    policy = DETECTOR_SCORING_POLICIES.get(detector)
   400→    if not policy or policy.dimension is None:
   401→        return None
   402→    return DIMENSIONS_BY_NAME.get(policy.dimension)
   403→
   404→
   405→__all__ = [
   406→    "ScoreBundle",
   407→    "compute_dimension_scores_by_mode",
   408→    "compute_dimension_scores",
   409→    "compute_health_breakdown",
   410→    "compute_health_score",
   411→    "compute_score_bundle",
   412→    "compute_score_impact",
   413→    "get_dimension_for_detector",
   414→]
   415→
```

```
1→"""Per-detector scoring calculations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import (
     8→    CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS,
     9→    FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE,
    10→    HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER,
    11→    SCORING_MODES,
    12→    ScoreMode,
    13→    detector_policy,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import Finding
    16→
    17→# Tiered file-count cap thresholds for non-LOC file-based detectors.
    18→# Controls how many findings per file contribute to the weighted failure sum.
    19→_FILE_CAP_HIGH_THRESHOLD = 6     # findings in file for high cap
    20→_FILE_CAP_MID_THRESHOLD = 3      # findings in file for mid cap
    21→_FILE_CAP_HIGH = 2.0             # cap value at high concentration
    22→_FILE_CAP_MID = 1.5              # cap value at mid concentration
    23→_FILE_CAP_LOW = 1.0              # cap value at low concentration (1-2 findings)
    24→
    25→
    26→def merge_potentials(potentials_by_lang: dict[str, dict[str, int]]) -> dict[str, int]:
    27→    """Sum potentials across languages per detector."""
    28→    merged: dict[str, int] = {}
    29→    for lang_potentials in potentials_by_lang.values():
    30→        for detector, count in lang_potentials.items():
    31→            merged[detector] = merged.get(detector, 0) + count
    32→    return merged
    33→
    34→
    35→def _iter_scoring_candidates(
    36→    detector: str,
    37→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    38→    excluded_zones: frozenset[str],
    39→):
    40→    """Yield in-scope findings for a detector (zone-filtered)."""
    41→    for finding in findings.values():
    42→        if finding.get("detector") != detector:
    43→            continue
    44→        if finding.get("zone", "production") in excluded_zones:
    45→            continue
    46→        yield finding
    47→
    48→
    49→def _finding_weight(finding: Finding, *, use_loc_weight: bool) -> float:
    50→    """Compute the scoring weight for a single finding."""
    51→    if use_loc_weight:
    52→        return finding.get("detail", {}).get("loc_weight", 1.0)
    53→    return CONFIDENCE_WEIGHTS.get(finding.get("confidence", "medium"), 0.7)
    54→
    55→
    56→def _file_count_cap(findings_in_file: int) -> float:
    57→    """Tiered cap for non-LOC file-based detectors.
    58→
    59→    Keeps file-count denominator semantics while preserving concentration signal:
    60→    1-2 findings => _FILE_CAP_LOW, 3-5 => _FILE_CAP_MID, 6+ => _FILE_CAP_HIGH.
    61→    """
    62→    if findings_in_file >= _FILE_CAP_HIGH_THRESHOLD:
    63→        return _FILE_CAP_HIGH
    64→    if findings_in_file >= _FILE_CAP_MID_THRESHOLD:
    65→        return _FILE_CAP_MID
    66→    return _FILE_CAP_LOW
    67→
    68→
    69→@dataclass
    70→class _ModeAccum:
    71→    """Per-mode accumulator for file-based detector scoring."""
    72→
    73→    by_file: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    74→    by_file_count: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)
    75→    file_cap: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    76→    holistic_sum: float = 0.0
    77→    issue_count: int = 0
    78→
    79→
    80→def _file_based_failures_by_mode(
    81→    detector: str,
    82→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    83→    policy,
    84→) -> dict[ScoreMode, tuple[int, float]]:
    85→    """Accumulate weighted failures by score mode for file-based detectors."""
    86→    accum: dict[ScoreMode, _ModeAccum] = {mode: _ModeAccum() for mode in SCORING_MODES}
    87→
    88→    for finding in _iter_scoring_candidates(detector, findings, policy.excluded_zones):
    89→        status = finding.get("status", "open")
    90→        holistic = finding.get("file") == "." and finding.get("detail", {}).get(
    91→            "holistic"
    92→        )
    93→
    94→        for mode in SCORING_MODES:
    95→            if status not in FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode]:
    96→                continue
    97→
    98→            if holistic:
    99→                accum[mode].holistic_sum += (
   100→                    _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=False) * HOLISTIC_MULTIPLIER
   101→                )
   102→                accum[mode].issue_count += 1
   103→                continue
   104→
   105→            weight = _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=policy.use_loc_weight)
   106→            file_key = finding.get("file", "")
   107→            a = accum[mode]
   108→            a.by_file[file_key] = a.by_file.get(file_key, 0.0) + weight
   109→            a.by_file_count[file_key] = a.by_file_count.get(file_key, 0) + 1
   110→            if policy.use_loc_weight and file_key not in a.file_cap:
   111→                a.file_cap[file_key] = weight
   112→            a.issue_count += 1
   113→
   114→    out: dict[ScoreMode, tuple[int, float]] = {}
   115→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   116→        a = accum[mode]
   117→        if policy.use_loc_weight:
   118→            weighted = sum(
   119→                min(weighted_sum, a.file_cap.get(file_key, weighted_sum))
   120→                for file_key, weighted_sum in a.by_file.items()
   121→            )
   122→        else:
   123→            weighted = sum(
   124→                min(weighted_sum, _file_count_cap(a.by_file_count.get(file_key, 0)))
   125→                for file_key, weighted_sum in a.by_file.items()
   126→            )
   127→        out[mode] = (a.issue_count, weighted + a.holistic_sum)
   128→    return out
   129→
   130→
   131→def detector_stats_by_mode(
   132→    detector: str,
   133→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
   134→    potential: int,
   135→) -> dict[ScoreMode, tuple[float, int, float]]:
   136→    """Compute (pass_rate, issue_count, weighted_failures) for each score mode."""
   137→    if potential <= 0:
   138→        return {mode: (1.0, 0, 0.0) for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   139→
   140→    # Review and concern findings are scored via subjective assessments only —
   141→    # exclude them from the detection-side scoring pipeline so resolving these
   142→    # findings never changes the score directly.
   143→    if detector in ("review", "concerns"):
   144→        return {mode: (1.0, 0, 0.0) for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   145→
   146→    policy = detector_policy(detector)
   147→
   148→    if policy.file_based:
   149→        mode_failures = _file_based_failures_by_mode(detector, findings, policy)
   150→    else:
   151→        issue_count: dict[ScoreMode, int] = {mode: 0 for mode in SCORING_MODES}
   152→        weighted_failures: dict[ScoreMode, float] = {
   153→            mode: 0.0 for mode in SCORING_MODES
   154→        }
   155→
   156→        for finding in _iter_scoring_candidates(
   157→            detector, findings, policy.excluded_zones
   158→        ):
   159→            status = finding.get("status", "open")
   160→            weight = _finding_weight(finding, use_loc_weight=False)
   161→            for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   162→                if status not in FAILURE_STATUSES_BY_MODE[mode]:
   163→                    continue
   164→                issue_count[mode] += 1
   165→                weighted_failures[mode] += weight
   166→
   167→        mode_failures = {
   168→            mode: (issue_count[mode], weighted_failures[mode]) for mode in SCORING_MODES
   169→        }
   170→
   171→    out: dict[ScoreMode, tuple[float, int, float]] = {}
   172→    for mode in SCORING_MODES:
   173→        issues, weighted = mode_failures[mode]
   174→        pass_rate = max(0.0, (potential - weighted) / potential)
   175→        out[mode] = (pass_rate, issues, weighted)
   176→    return out
   177→
   178→
   179→def detector_pass_rate(
   180→    detector: str,
   181→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
   182→    potential: int,
   183→    *,
   184→    strict: bool = False,
   185→) -> tuple[float, int, float]:
   186→    """Pass rate for one detector.
   187→
   188→    Returns (pass_rate, issue_count, weighted_failures).
   189→    Zero potential -> (1.0, 0, 0.0).
   190→    """
   191→    mode: ScoreMode = "strict" if strict else "lenient"
   192→    return detector_stats_by_mode(detector, findings, potential)[mode]
   193→
   194→
   195→__all__ = [
   196→    "detector_pass_rate",
   197→    "detector_stats_by_mode",
   198→    "merge_potentials",
   199→]
   200→
```

```
1→"""Subjective-dimension metadata derived from review dimension payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→from functools import lru_cache
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     9→from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.data import (
    10→    load_dimensions,
    11→    load_dimensions_for_lang,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    14→from desloppify.scoring import DISPLAY_NAMES
    15→
    16→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    17→
    18→# Canonical display names — imported from engine/_scoring/subjective/core.py.
    19→_LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES: dict[str, str] = DISPLAY_NAMES
    20→
    21→_LEGACY_SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHTS_BY_DISPLAY: dict[str, float] = {
    22→    "high elegance": 22.0,
    23→    "mid elegance": 22.0,
    24→    "low elegance": 12.0,
    25→    "contracts": 12.0,
    26→    "type safety": 12.0,
    27→    "abstraction fit": 8.0,
    28→    "logic clarity": 6.0,
    29→    "structure nav": 5.0,
    30→    "error consistency": 3.0,
    31→    "naming quality": 2.0,
    32→    "ai generated debt": 1.0,
    33→}
    34→
    35→_LEGACY_RESET_ON_SCAN_DIMENSIONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(
    36→    {
    37→        "naming_quality",
    38→        "error_consistency",
    39→        "abstraction_fitness",
    40→        "logic_clarity",
    41→        "ai_generated_debt",
    42→        "type_safety",
    43→        "contract_coherence",
    44→        "package_organization",
    45→        "high_level_elegance",
    46→        "mid_level_elegance",
    47→        "low_level_elegance",
    48→    }
    49→)
    50→
    51→_LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION: dict[str, float] = {}
    52→for _dimension_key, _display_name in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
    53→    _weight = _LEGACY_SUBJECTIVE_WEIGHTS_BY_DISPLAY.get(
    54→        " ".join(_display_name.strip().lower().split())
    55→    )
    56→    if _weight is not None:
    57→        _LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION[_dimension_key] = _weight
    58→
    59→
    60→def _normalize_dimension_name(name: str) -> str:
    61→    return "_".join(str(name).strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    62→
    63→
    64→def _title_display_name(dimension_key: str) -> str:
    65→    return dimension_key.replace("_", " ").title()
    66→
    67→
    68→def _normalize_lang_name(lang_name: str | None) -> str | None:
    69→    if not isinstance(lang_name, str):
    70→        return None
    71→    cleaned = lang_name.strip().lower()
    72→    return cleaned or None
    73→
    74→
    75→def _extract_prompt_meta(entry: object) -> dict[str, object]:
    76→    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    77→        return {}
    78→    meta = entry.get("meta")
    79→    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
    80→        return {}
    81→    out: dict[str, object] = {}
    82→
    83→    if isinstance(meta.get("display_name"), str) and meta["display_name"].strip():
    84→        out["display_name"] = meta["display_name"].strip()
    85→
    86→    weight = meta.get("weight")
    87→    if is_numeric(weight):
    88→        out["weight"] = max(0.0, float(weight))
    89→
    90→    enabled = meta.get("enabled_by_default")
    91→    if isinstance(enabled, bool):
    92→        out["enabled_by_default"] = enabled
    93→
    94→    reset_on_scan = meta.get("reset_on_scan")
    95→    if isinstance(reset_on_scan, bool):
    96→        out["reset_on_scan"] = reset_on_scan
    97→
    98→    return out
    99→
   100→
   101→def _merge_dimension_meta(
   102→    target: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   103→    *,
   104→    dimensions: list[str],
   105→    prompts: dict[str, dict[str, object]],
   106→    override_existing: bool = False,
   107→) -> None:
   108→    """Merge one payload's prompt metadata into the shared registry."""
   109→    defaults = {
   110→        _normalize_dimension_name(dim)
   111→        for dim in dimensions
   112→        if isinstance(dim, str) and dim.strip()
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    for raw_dim, entry in prompts.items():
   116→        dim = _normalize_dimension_name(raw_dim)
   117→        if not dim:
   118→            continue
   119→
   120→        payload = target.setdefault(dim, {})
   121→        prompt_meta = _extract_prompt_meta(entry)
   122→
   123→        if "display_name" in prompt_meta and (
   124→            override_existing or "display_name" not in payload
   125→        ):
   126→            payload["display_name"] = prompt_meta["display_name"]
   127→        if "weight" in prompt_meta and (override_existing or "weight" not in payload):
   128→            payload["weight"] = prompt_meta["weight"]
   129→        if "reset_on_scan" in prompt_meta and (
   130→            override_existing or "reset_on_scan" not in payload
   131→        ):
   132→            payload["reset_on_scan"] = prompt_meta["reset_on_scan"]
   133→
   134→        if dim in defaults:
   135→            payload["enabled_by_default"] = True
   136→        if "enabled_by_default" in prompt_meta:
   137→            if override_existing:
   138→                payload["enabled_by_default"] = bool(prompt_meta["enabled_by_default"])
   139→            else:
   140→                payload["enabled_by_default"] = bool(
   141→                    payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)
   142→                    or prompt_meta["enabled_by_default"]
   143→                )
   144→
   145→
   146→def _available_languages() -> list[str]:
   147→    try:
   148→        return list(available_langs())
   149→    except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, RuntimeError):
   150→        return []
   151→
   152→
   153→def _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(
   154→    *,
   155→    lang_name: str | None,
   156→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   157→    """Build merged metadata for subjective dimensions."""
   158→    out: dict[str, dict[str, object]] = {}
   159→
   160→    shared_defaults, shared_prompts, _ = load_dimensions()
   161→    _merge_dimension_meta(out, dimensions=shared_defaults, prompts=shared_prompts)
   162→
   163→    langs = [lang_name] if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name.strip() else _available_languages()
   164→    for name in langs:
   165→        try:
   166→            lang_defaults, lang_prompts, _ = load_dimensions_for_lang(name)
   167→            _merge_dimension_meta(
   168→                out,
   169→                dimensions=lang_defaults,
   170→                prompts=lang_prompts,
   171→                override_existing=bool(lang_name),
   172→            )
   173→        except (ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
   174→            logger.debug("Failed to load dimensions for lang %s: %s", name, exc)
   175→            continue
   176→
   177→    for dim, payload in out.items():
   178→        payload.setdefault(
   179→            "display_name",
   180→            _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim, _title_display_name(dim)),
   181→        )
   182→        payload.setdefault("weight", _LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION.get(dim, 1.0))
   183→        payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", False)
   184→        if dim in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES:
   185→            payload.setdefault(
   186→                "reset_on_scan", dim in _LEGACY_RESET_ON_SCAN_DIMENSIONS
   187→            )
   188→        else:
   189→            payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", True)
   190→
   191→    # Preserve legacy dimensions even if a payload temporarily drops one.
   192→    for dim, display in _LEGACY_DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   193→        payload = out.setdefault(dim, {})
   194→        payload.setdefault("display_name", display)
   195→        payload.setdefault("weight", _LEGACY_WEIGHT_BY_DIMENSION.get(dim, 1.0))
   196→        payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", True)
   197→        payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", dim in _LEGACY_RESET_ON_SCAN_DIMENSIONS)
   198→
   199→    return out
   200→
   201→
   202→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   203→def load_subjective_dimension_metadata() -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   204→    """Return merged metadata across all known dimensions/languages."""
   205→    return _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(lang_name=None)
   206→
   207→
   208→@lru_cache(maxsize=16)
   209→def load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang(
   210→    lang_name: str | None,
   211→) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   212→    """Return merged metadata for one language (with language overrides)."""
   213→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   214→    return _build_subjective_dimension_metadata(lang_name=normalized)
   215→
   216→
   217→def _metadata_registry(lang_name: str | None) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
   218→    normalized = _normalize_lang_name(lang_name)
   219→    if normalized is None:
   220→        return load_subjective_dimension_metadata()
   221→    return load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang(normalized)
   222→
   223→
   224→def get_dimension_metadata(
   225→    dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None
   226→) -> dict[str, object]:
   227→    """Return metadata for one dimension key (with sane defaults)."""
   228→    dim = _normalize_dimension_name(dimension_name)
   229→    all_meta = _metadata_registry(lang_name)
   230→    payload = dict(all_meta.get(dim, {}))
   231→
   232→    payload.setdefault("display_name", _title_display_name(dim))
   233→    payload.setdefault("weight", 1.0)
   234→    payload.setdefault("enabled_by_default", False)
   235→    payload.setdefault("reset_on_scan", True)
   236→    return payload
   237→
   238→
   239→def dimension_display_name(dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None) -> str:
   240→    meta = get_dimension_metadata(dimension_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   241→    return str(meta.get("display_name", _title_display_name(dimension_name)))
   242→
   243→
   244→def dimension_weight(dimension_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None = None) -> float:
   245→    meta = get_dimension_metadata(dimension_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   246→    raw = meta.get("weight", 1.0)
   247→    if is_numeric(raw):
   248→        return max(0.0, float(raw))
   249→    return 1.0
   250→
   251→
   252→def default_display_names_map(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
   253→    """Display-name map for default subjective dimensions."""
   254→    out: dict[str, str] = {}
   255→    for dim, payload in _metadata_registry(lang_name).items():
   256→        if not bool(payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)):
   257→            continue
   258→        out[dim] = str(payload.get("display_name", _title_display_name(dim)))
   259→    return out
   260→
   261→
   262→def resettable_default_dimensions(*, lang_name: str | None = None) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   263→    """Default subjective dimensions that should be reset by scan reset."""
   264→    out = []
   265→    for dim, payload in _metadata_registry(lang_name).items():
   266→        if not bool(payload.get("enabled_by_default", False)):
   267→            continue
   268→        if not bool(payload.get("reset_on_scan", True)):
   269→            continue
   270→        out.append(dim)
   271→    return tuple(sorted(set(out)))
   272→
   273→
   274→__all__ = [
   275→    "default_display_names_map",
   276→    "dimension_display_name",
   277→    "dimension_weight",
   278→    "get_dimension_metadata",
   279→    "load_subjective_dimension_metadata",
   280→    "load_subjective_dimension_metadata_for_lang",
   281→    "resettable_default_dimensions",
   282→]
   283→
```

```
1→"""Subjective-dimension scoring helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
     6→from desloppify.engine._scoring.policy.core import SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS
     7→
     8→DISPLAY_NAMES: dict[str, str] = {
     9→    # Holistic dimensions
    10→    "cross_module_architecture": "Cross-module arch",
    11→    "initialization_coupling": "Init coupling",
    12→    "convention_outlier": "Convention drift",
    13→    "error_consistency": "Error consistency",
    14→    "abstraction_fitness": "Abstraction fit",
    15→    "dependency_health": "Dep health",
    16→    "test_strategy": "Test strategy",
    17→    "api_surface_coherence": "API coherence",
    18→    "authorization_consistency": "Auth consistency",
    19→    "ai_generated_debt": "AI generated debt",
    20→    "incomplete_migration": "Stale migration",
    21→    "package_organization": "Structure nav",
    22→    "high_level_elegance": "High elegance",
    23→    "mid_level_elegance": "Mid elegance",
    24→    "low_level_elegance": "Low elegance",
    25→    # Design coherence (concerns bridge)
    26→    "design_coherence": "Design coherence",
    27→    # Per-file review dimensions
    28→    "naming_quality": "Naming quality",
    29→    "logic_clarity": "Logic clarity",
    30→    "type_safety": "Type safety",
    31→    "contract_coherence": "Contracts",
    32→}
    33→
    34→def _display_fallback(dim_name: str) -> str:
    35→    words = dim_name.replace("_", " ")
    36→    return words[0].upper() + words[1:] if words else words
    37→
    38→
    39→def _normalize_dimension_key(dim_name: object) -> str:
    40→    if not isinstance(dim_name, str):
    41→        return ""
    42→    return "_".join(dim_name.strip().lower().replace("-", "_").split())
    43→
    44→
    45→def _primary_lang_from_findings(findings: dict) -> str | None:
    46→    counts: dict[str, int] = {}
    47→    for finding in findings.values():
    48→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
    49→            continue
    50→        raw_lang = finding.get("lang")
    51→        if not isinstance(raw_lang, str) or not raw_lang.strip():
    52→            continue
    53→        key = raw_lang.strip().lower()
    54→        counts[key] = counts.get(key, 0) + 1
    55→    if not counts:
    56→        return None
    57→    return max(counts, key=counts.get)
    58→
    59→
    60→def _dimension_display_name(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> str:
    61→    try:
    62→        # Deferred import to avoid circular: scoring -> _scoring/results/core
    63→        # -> _scoring/subjective/core -> intelligence.review -> state -> scoring
    64→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    65→            dimension_display_name,
    66→        )
    67→
    68→        return str(dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    69→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    70→        return DISPLAY_NAMES.get(dim_name, _display_fallback(dim_name))
    71→
    72→
    73→def _dimension_weight(dim_name: str, *, lang_name: str | None) -> float:
    74→    try:
    75→        # Deferred import to avoid circular (see _dimension_display_name).
    76→        from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.metadata import (
    77→            dimension_weight,
    78→        )
    79→
    80→        return float(dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name))
    81→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError, ValueError, TypeError):
    82→        return 1.0
    83→
    84→
    85→def append_subjective_dimensions(
    86→    results: dict,
    87→    findings: dict,
    88→    assessments: dict | None,
    89→    failure_set: frozenset[str],
    90→    allowed_dimensions: set[str] | None = None,
    91→) -> None:
    92→    """Append subjective review dimensions to results dict (mutates results).
    93→
    94→    Subjective scoring is evidence-first: open review findings for a dimension
    95→    determine pass-rate, while imported assessment scores are retained as
    96→    metadata for transparency.
    97→    """
    98→    # Deferred import to avoid circular (see _dimension_display_name).
    99→    from desloppify.intelligence.review.dimensions.holistic import DIMENSIONS
   100→
   101→    raw_defaults = DIMENSIONS
   102→    allowed = (
   103→        {_normalize_dimension_key(name) for name in allowed_dimensions}
   104→        if allowed_dimensions is not None
   105→        else None
   106→    )
   107→
   108→    default_dimensions = []
   109→    for raw_dim in raw_defaults:
   110→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   111→        if not dim:
   112→            continue
   113→        if allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   114→            continue
   115→        default_dimensions.append(dim)
   116→
   117→    assessed: dict[str, dict] = {}
   118→    for raw_dim, payload in (assessments or {}).items():
   119→        dim = _normalize_dimension_key(raw_dim)
   120→        if not dim:
   121→            continue
   122→        # Real assessments (from review imports) always count regardless of
   123→        # allowed_dimensions — they were deliberately imported and should not
   124→        # be silently discarded.  However, system-generated placeholders
   125→        # (scan_reset_subjective) are not real assessments and should respect
   126→        # the allowed_dimensions scope like defaults.
   127→        is_placeholder = isinstance(payload, dict) and (
   128→            payload.get("placeholder") is True
   129→            or payload.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   130→            or payload.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   131→        )
   132→        if is_placeholder and allowed is not None and dim not in allowed:
   133→            continue
   134→        assessed[dim] = payload
   135→    existing_lower = {k.lower() for k in results}
   136→    lang_name = _primary_lang_from_findings(findings)
   137→
   138→    all_dims = list(default_dimensions)
   139→    for dim_name in assessed:
   140→        if dim_name not in default_dimensions:
   141→            all_dims.append(dim_name)
   142→
   143→    for dim_name in all_dims:
   144→        is_default = dim_name in default_dimensions
   145→        assessment = assessed.get(dim_name)
   146→        has_assessment = isinstance(assessment, dict)
   147→        if not is_default and not assessment:
   148→            continue
   149→
   150→        display = _dimension_display_name(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name)
   151→        if display.lower() in existing_lower:
   152→            display = f"{display} (subjective)"
   153→
   154→        # Count open review/concern findings for display (work queue), but
   155→        # these do NOT drive the dimension score — only assessment scores do.
   156→        issue_count = sum(
   157→            1
   158→            for finding in findings.values()
   159→            if finding.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   160→            and finding.get("status") in failure_set
   161→            and _normalize_dimension_key(
   162→                finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension")
   163→            )
   164→            == dim_name
   165→        )
   166→
   167→        assessment_score = (
   168→            max(0.0, min(100.0, float(assessment.get("score", 0))))
   169→            if isinstance(assessment, dict)
   170→            else 0.0
   171→        )
   172→        integrity_penalty = (
   173→            assessment.get("integrity_penalty")
   174→            if isinstance(assessment, dict)
   175→            else None
   176→        )
   177→        reset_pending = bool(
   178→            isinstance(assessment, dict)
   179→            and (
   180→                assessment.get("reset_by") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   181→                or assessment.get("source") == "scan_reset_subjective"
   182→                or assessment.get("placeholder") is True
   183→            )
   184→        )
   185→        if reset_pending:
   186→            score = 0.0
   187→            pass_rate = 0.0
   188→        elif integrity_penalty == "target_match_reset":
   189→            score = 0.0
   190→            pass_rate = 0.0
   191→        elif has_assessment:
   192→            # Assessment score drives the dimension score directly.
   193→            # Resolving review findings does NOT change this score —
   194→            # only a fresh review import updates it.
   195→            score = assessment_score
   196→            pass_rate = score / 100.0
   197→        else:
   198→            # No assessment yet: explicit unassessed placeholder.
   199→            # Subjective dimensions start at 0 until an assessment is imported.
   200→            score = 0.0
   201→            pass_rate = 0.0
   202→        components: list[str] = []
   203→        component_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
   204→        if isinstance(assessment, dict):
   205→            raw_components = assessment.get("components")
   206→            if isinstance(raw_components, list):
   207→                components = [
   208→                    str(item).strip()
   209→                    for item in raw_components
   210→                    if isinstance(item, str) and item.strip()
   211→                ]
   212→            raw_component_scores = assessment.get("component_scores")
   213→            if isinstance(raw_component_scores, dict):
   214→                for key, value in raw_component_scores.items():
   215→                    if not isinstance(key, str) or not key.strip():
   216→                        continue
   217→                    if not is_numeric(value):
   218→                        continue
   219→                    component_scores[key.strip()] = round(
   220→                        max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))),
   221→                        1,
   222→                    )
   223→
   224→        results[display] = {
   225→            "score": round(float(score), 1),
   226→            "tier": 4,
   227→            "checks": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   228→            "issues": issue_count,
   229→            "detectors": {
   230→                "subjective_assessment": {
   231→                    "potential": SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS,
   232→                    "pass_rate": round(pass_rate, 4),
   233→                    "issues": issue_count,
   234→                    "weighted_failures": round(SUBJECTIVE_CHECKS * (1 - pass_rate), 4),
   235→                    "assessment_score": round(assessment_score, 1),
   236→                    "placeholder": reset_pending or not has_assessment,
   237→                    "dimension_key": dim_name,
   238→                    "configured_weight": round(
   239→                        _dimension_weight(dim_name, lang_name=lang_name), 6
   240→                    ),
   241→                    "components": components,
   242→                    **(
   243→                        {"component_scores": component_scores}
   244→                        if component_scores
   245→                        else {}
   246→                    ),
   247→                }
   248→            },
   249→        }
   250→
   251→
   252→__all__ = [
   253→    "DISPLAY_NAMES",
   254→    "append_subjective_dimensions",
   255→]
   256→
```

# Desloppify UX Feedback Report: "How do I improve my score?"

## Context

I am a fresh user who just ran a scan and got a strict score of **90.6/100** against a target of **98.0**. I wanted to know: which specific issues should I fix for the biggest score improvement, and how much will each fix help?

---

## 1. Impact Visibility: **3/10**

**Could I see how much fixing something would help my score?**

No. At no point did any command tell me "fixing X will gain you Y points toward your strict score." The scan output shows a "Biggest weighted drags" section:

```
Biggest weighted drags:
  - Contracts: -2.05 pts (score 65.0%, 9.8% of subjective pool)
  - Abstraction fit: -2.03 pts (score 48.0%, 6.5% of subjective pool)
  - File health: -1.24 pts (score 90.7%, 33.3% of mechanical pool)
  - Type safety: -0.53 pts (score 91.0%, 9.8% of subjective pool)
  - Error consistency: -0.51 pts (score 65.3%, 2.4% of subjective pool)
```

This tells me which *dimensions* are dragging me down and by how many *weighted points*, which is genuinely useful at the dimension level. But when I then use `next` or `next --explain` to find the *specific finding* to fix, there is zero per-finding impact information. The `--explain` output for T1 says:

```
explain: ranked by tier=1, confidence=high, count=0, id=review::...
```

And for T2 it says:

```
explain: ranked by tier=2, confidence=high, count=0, id=dict_keys::... Dimension: Code quality at 96.9% (220 open issues)
```

The T2 explain line is better because it at least names the dimension and its current health. But neither tells me "fixing this one finding will improve your strict score by approximately X points." I am left doing mental math: if Contracts is dragging by -2.05 pts and has 3 open findings, fixing one *might* recover ~0.68 pts? But this is a guess. The tool should do this math for me.

**What's missing**: Per-finding score impact estimate, e.g., "+0.7 pts on strict score" or "moves Contracts from 65.0% to ~76.7%."

---

## 2. Drag Summary Usefulness: **7/10**

**Did the "biggest score drags" in scan output help me prioritize?**

Yes, significantly. The drag summary is the single most useful piece of prioritization information in the entire output. It told me clearly:

- **Contracts** (-2.05 pts) and **Abstraction fit** (-2.03 pts) are my two biggest drags, despite being subjective dimensions with relatively few findings (3 and 4 respectively).
- **File health** (-1.24 pts) is the largest mechanical drag.
- The score recipe breakdown (`overall = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective`) was essential for understanding why low-scoring subjective dimensions with few findings matter more than I would have guessed.

**What's missing**: The drag summary lives in `scan` output only. It does not appear in `next`, `plan`, or anywhere else where I am actively deciding what to fix. The `next` command should echo "This finding is in the Contracts dimension, which is your #1 drag at -2.05 pts" instead of just saying "tier=1, confidence=high."

---

## 3. T1 Label Accuracy: **6/10**

**Does the T1 label correctly describe what's in T1?**

The `plan` output labels T1 as:

```
Tier 1 (Highest priority (auto-fixable + high-confidence holistic review)): 6 open / 172 total (97% addressed)
```

Looking at the actual T1 items:
- 5 are holistic review findings (subjective, requiring judgment -- e.g., "TypedDict-backed payload/state boundaries are softened by `.get()` + default coercion")
- 1 is an unused import (`typing.Any`) which is genuinely auto-fixable

So the label says "auto-fixable + high-confidence holistic review" but 5 of 6 items are holistic review items that require design judgment, not auto-fixable at all. The `next` command itself labels the top T1 item as:

```
Type: Design review (requires judgment)
```

This is honest and accurate on a per-finding basis, but the tier label at the plan level is misleading. The "auto-fixable" part of the label only applies to 1 of the 6 items. A user skimming the plan might think T1 is quick wins they can auto-fix, when really it is mostly subjective design review work.

**What would be better**: "Highest priority (high-confidence review findings + auto-fixable)" or dynamically composing the label based on what is actually in the tier.

---

## 4. Explain Depth: **4/10**

**Did `--explain` give dimension context or just "tier=1, confidence=high"?**

For T1:
```
explain: ranked by tier=1, confidence=high, count=0, id=review::.::holistic::abstraction_fitness::typed_dict_contract_softening::5e16e1c8
```

This is essentially just a restatement of the sort key. It tells me the ranking *criteria* but not the *reasoning*. I already knew it was T1/high from the header. The `count=0` is presumably fix attempts but is not explained. The ID is not useful context.

For T2 it was slightly better:
```
explain: ranked by tier=2, confidence=high, count=0, id=dict_keys::... Dimension: Code quality at 96.9% (220 open issues)
```

The dimension name and health percentage are genuinely useful here, but still missing: why this specific finding over the other 92 T2 items, what the score impact would be, and whether this feeds into the "biggest drags."

**What I wanted to see**:
- "This finding is in the **Abstraction fit** dimension (48.0%), which is your **#2 biggest drag** at -2.03 pts. There are 4 open findings in this dimension; resolving this one could improve the dimension to ~61.5% and recover approximately +0.5 pts on your strict score."
- Or at minimum: "Prioritized because: T1 tier, high confidence, targets Abstraction fit (48.0% -- below 95% target, 2nd largest drag on overall score)."

---

## 5. Prioritization Clarity: **5/10**

**Could I figure out what to fix FIRST for maximum score impact?**

With effort, yes, but the tool made me work too hard for it. Here is my reconstruction:

1. From `scan` I learned that Contracts (-2.05 pts) and Abstraction fit (-2.03 pts) are the top drags.
2. From `plan` I can see Abstraction fit has 4 open findings and Contracts has 3 open findings, all in T4 (subjective review).
3. But from `next --tier 1` I got an Abstraction fit finding presented as T1. Confusingly, the same dimension appears in T1 (holistic review findings) and T4 (in the plan dimension table).
4. The `next` command does show a "North star" line: `strict 90.6/100 -> target 98.0 (+7.4 needed)` which is good for context but does not help me choose *which* item to fix.

The fundamental gap: the tool never connects the per-finding prioritization to the per-dimension score impact. I can see the drag table. I can see the next finding. But I cannot see: "Of all 743 open findings, here are the 5 that would collectively recover the most points, and here is what your score would be after fixing them."

**What would make this a 9/10**: A `next --top 5` or `plan --projection` mode that shows: "Fix these 5 items in order -> estimated score trajectory: 90.6 -> 92.1 -> 93.4 -> ..."

---

## 6. Score Legend Presence: **7/10**

**Did I see and understand the score guide (overall/strict/objective/verified)?**

The scan output shows:

```
Scores: overall 90.6/100  objective 94.9/100  strict 90.6/100  verified 94.9/100
```

And the score recipe section explains:

```
Score recipe:
  overall = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective
  Pool averages: mechanical 94.9% . subjective 87.7%
```

The plan header shows:

```
Health: overall 90.6/100 | objective 94.9/100 | strict 90.6/100 | 743 open | 0 fixed | 0 wontfix | 11331 auto-resolved
```

The recipe is helpful. But I still do not fully understand the difference between "overall" vs "strict" vs "objective" vs "verified." The scan output shows overall=90.6 and strict=90.6 (same value), and objective=94.9 and verified=94.9 (also same value). Without a legend explaining what each one *means*, I can only guess:
- "objective" = mechanical detectors only?
- "strict" = includes stale subjective scores without discounting?
- "verified" = only non-stale verified dimensions?
- "overall" = the blended score?

The tool never explicitly defines these four terms. The recipe only explains "overall." A brief legend like "strict = overall but treating stale subjective dimensions at face value" would help enormously.

---

## Summary Table

| Criterion | Rating | Key Quote |
|---|---|---|
| **Impact visibility** | 3/10 | `explain: ranked by tier=1, confidence=high, count=0` -- no per-finding score impact |
| **Drag summary usefulness** | 7/10 | `Contracts: -2.05 pts (score 65.0%, 9.8% of subjective pool)` -- genuinely useful |
| **T1 label accuracy** | 6/10 | Label says "auto-fixable + high-confidence holistic review" but 5/6 items are design review |
| **Explain depth** | 4/10 | T2 shows dimension name/health; T1 does not; neither shows score impact |
| **Prioritization clarity** | 5/10 | Can reconstruct priority from drag table + plan, but tool does not connect them |
| **Score legend presence** | 7/10 | Recipe for "overall" is clear; other 3 score types (strict/objective/verified) are undefined |

**Overall UX grade: 5.3/10**

## The Single Biggest Gap

The tool is excellent at *diagnosing* problems (the dimension table, the drag summary, the detector-level output). It is weak at answering the actionable question: **"If I fix this specific thing, how much will my score improve?"** The drag summary gives dimension-level impact, but the `next` and `plan` commands never translate that into per-finding expected score change. A user asking "what should I fix first for maximum score improvement?" has to mentally cross-reference three different command outputs and do their own arithmetic. The tool should do that math and present it directly in `next --explain`.
